/**
  ******************************************************************************
  * @file    system_stm32f1xx.c
  * @author  MCD Application Team
  * @brief   CMSIS Cortex-M3 Device Peripheral Access Layer System Source File.
  * 
  * 1.  This file provides two functions and one global variable to be called from 
  *     user application:
  *      - SystemInit(): Setups the system clock (System clock source, PLL Multiplier
  *                      factors, AHB/APBx prescalers and Flash settings). 
  *                      This function is called at startup just after reset and 
  *                      before branch to main program. This call is made inside
  *                      the "startup_stm32f1xx_xx.s" file.
  *
  *      - SystemCoreClock variable: Contains the core clock (HCLK), it can be used
  *                                  by the user application to setup the SysTick 
  *                                  timer or configure other parameters.
  *                                     
  *      - SystemCoreClockUpdate(): Updates the variable SystemCoreClock and must
  *                                 be called whenever the core clock is changed
  *                                 during program execution.
  *
  * 2. After each device reset the HSI (8 MHz) is used as system clock source.
  *    Then SystemInit() function is called, in "startup_stm32f1xx_xx.s" file, to
  *    configure the system clock before to branch to main program.
  *
  * 4. The default value of HSE crystal is set to 8 MHz (or 25 MHz, depending on
  *    the product used), refer to "HSE_VALUE". 
  *    When HSE is used as system clock source, directly or through PLL, and you
  *    are using different crystal you have to adapt the HSE value to your own
  *    configuration.
  *        
  ******************************************************************************
  * @attention
  *
  * Copyright (c) 2017-2021 STMicroelectronics.
  * All rights reserved.
  *
  * This software is licensed under terms that can be found in the LICENSE file
  * in the root directory of this software component.
  * If no LICENSE file comes with this software, it is provided AS-IS.
  *
  ******************************************************************************
  */

/** @addtogroup CMSIS
  * @{
  */

/** @addtogroup stm32f1xx_system
  * @{
  */

                                                This value can be provided and adapted by the user application. */

#if !defined  (HSI_VALUE)

                                                This value can be provided and adapted by the user application. */
#endif /* HSI_VALUE */

  /* This variable is updated in three ways:
      1) by calling CMSIS function SystemCoreClockUpdate()
      2) by calling HAL API function HAL_RCC_GetHCLKFreq()
      3) each time HAL_RCC_ClockConfig() is called to configure the system clock frequency 
         Note: If you use this function to configure the system clock; then there
               is no need to call the 2 first functions listed above, since SystemCoreClock
               variable is updated automatically.
  */
uint32_t SystemCoreClock = 8000000;
const uint8_t AHBPrescTable[16U] = {0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 7, 8, 9};
const uint8_t APBPrescTable[8U] =  {0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4};

/**

  *         Initialize the Embedded Flash Interface, the PLL and update the 
  *         SystemCoreClock variable.
  * @note   This function should be used only after reset.
  * @param  None

  */
void SystemInit (void)
{
#if defined(STM32F100xE) || defined(STM32F101xE) || defined(STM32F101xG) || defined(STM32F103xE) || defined(STM32F103xG)
  #ifdef DATA_IN_ExtSRAM
    SystemInit_ExtMemCtl(); 
  #endif /* DATA_IN_ExtSRAM */
#endif 

  /* Configure the Vector Table location -------------------------------------*/
#if defined(USER_VECT_TAB_ADDRESS)
  SCB->VTOR = VECT_TAB_BASE_ADDRESS | VECT_TAB_OFFSET; /* Vector Table Relocation in Internal SRAM. */
#endif /* USER_VECT_TAB_ADDRESS */
}

/**
  * @brief  Update SystemCoreClock variable according to Clock Register Values.
  *         The SystemCoreClock variable contains the core clock (HCLK), it can
  *         be used by the user application to setup the SysTick timer or configure
  *         other parameters.
  *           
  * @note   Each time the core clock (HCLK) changes, this function must be called
  *         to update SystemCoreClock variable value. Otherwise, any configuration
  *         based on this variable will be incorrect.         
  *     
  * @note   - The system frequency computed by this function is not the real 
  *           frequency in the chip. It is calculated based on the predefined 
  *           constant and the selected clock source:
  *             
  *           - If SYSCLK source is HSI, SystemCoreClock will contain the HSI_VALUE(*)
  *                                              
  *           - If SYSCLK source is HSE, SystemCoreClock will contain the HSE_VALUE(**)
  *                          
  *           - If SYSCLK source is PLL, SystemCoreClock will contain the HSE_VALUE(**) 
  *             or HSI_VALUE(*) multiplied by the PLL factors.
  *         
  *         (*) HSI_VALUE is a constant defined in stm32f1xx.h file (default value
  *             8 MHz) but the real value may vary depending on the variations
  *             in voltage and temperature.   
  *    
  *         (**) HSE_VALUE is a constant defined in stm32f1xx.h file (default value
  *              8 MHz or 25 MHz, depending on the product used), user has to ensure
  *              that HSE_VALUE is same as the real frequency of the crystal used.
  *              Otherwise, this function may have wrong result.
  *                
  *         - The result of this function could be not correct when using fractional
  *           value for HSE crystal.
  * @param  None
  * @retval None
  */
void SystemCoreClockUpdate (void)
{
  uint32_t tmp = 0U, pllmull = 0U, pllsource = 0U;

#if defined(STM32F105xC) || defined(STM32F107xC)
  uint32_t prediv1source = 0U, prediv1factor = 0U, prediv2factor = 0U, pll2mull = 0U;
#endif /* STM32F105xC */

#if defined(STM32F100xB) || defined(STM32F100xE)
  uint32_t prediv1factor = 0U;
#endif /* STM32F100xB or STM32F100xE */
    
  /* Get SYSCLK source -------------------------------------------------------*/
  tmp = RCC->CFGR & RCC_CFGR_SWS;
  
  switch (tmp)
  {
    case 0x00U:  /* HSI used as system clock */
      SystemCoreClock = HSI_VALUE;
      break;
    case 0x04U:  /* HSE used as system clock */
      SystemCoreClock = HSE_VALUE;
      break;
    case 0x08U:  /* PLL used as system clock */

      /* Get PLL clock source and multiplication factor ----------------------*/
      pllmull = RCC->CFGR & RCC_CFGR_PLLMULL;
      pllsource = RCC->CFGR & RCC_CFGR_PLLSRC;
      
#if !defined(STM32F105xC) && !defined(STM32F107xC)      
      pllmull = ( pllmull >> 18U) + 2U;
      
      if (pllsource == 0x00U)
      {
        /* HSI oscillator clock divided by 2 selected as PLL clock entry */
        SystemCoreClock = (HSI_VALUE >> 1U) * pllmull;
      }
      else
      {
 #if defined(STM32F100xB) || defined(STM32F100xE)
       prediv1factor = (RCC->CFGR2 & RCC_CFGR2_PREDIV1) + 1U;
       /* HSE oscillator clock selected as PREDIV1 clock entry */
       SystemCoreClock = (HSE_VALUE / prediv1factor) * pllmull; 
 #else
        /* HSE selected as PLL clock entry */
        if ((RCC->CFGR & RCC_CFGR_PLLXTPRE) != (uint32_t)RESET)
        {/* HSE oscillator clock divided by 2 */
          SystemCoreClock = (HSE_VALUE >> 1U) * pllmull;
        }
        else
        {
          SystemCoreClock = HSE_VALUE * pllmull;
        }
 #endif
      }
#else
      pllmull = pllmull >> 18U;
      
      if (pllmull != 0x0DU)
      {
         pllmull += 2U;
      }
      else
      { /* PLL multiplication factor = PLL input clock * 6.5 */
        pllmull = 13U / 2U; 
      }
            
      if (pllsource == 0x00U)
      {
        /* HSI oscillator clock divided by 2 selected as PLL clock entry */
        SystemCoreClock = (HSI_VALUE >> 1U) * pllmull;
      }
      else
      {/* PREDIV1 selected as PLL clock entry */
        
        /* Get PREDIV1 clock source and division factor */
        prediv1source = RCC->CFGR2 & RCC_CFGR2_PREDIV1SRC;
        prediv1factor = (RCC->CFGR2 & RCC_CFGR2_PREDIV1) + 1U;
        
        if (prediv1source == 0U)
        { 
          /* HSE oscillator clock selected as PREDIV1 clock entry */
          SystemCoreClock = (HSE_VALUE / prediv1factor) * pllmull;          
        }
        else
        {/* PLL2 clock selected as PREDIV1 clock entry */
          
          /* Get PREDIV2 division factor and PLL2 multiplication factor */
          prediv2factor = ((RCC->CFGR2 & RCC_CFGR2_PREDIV2) >> 4U) + 1U;
          pll2mull = ((RCC->CFGR2 & RCC_CFGR2_PLL2MUL) >> 8U) + 2U; 
          SystemCoreClock = (((HSE_VALUE / prediv2factor) * pll2mull) / prediv1factor) * pllmull;                         
        }
      }
#endif /* STM32F105xC */ 
      break;

    default:
      SystemCoreClock = HSI_VALUE;
      break;
  }
  
  /* Compute HCLK clock frequency ----------------*/
  /* Get HCLK prescaler */
  tmp = AHBPrescTable[((RCC->CFGR & RCC_CFGR_HPRE) >> 4U)];
  /* HCLK clock frequency */
  SystemCoreClock >>= tmp;  
}

#if defined(STM32F100xE) || defined(STM32F101xE) || defined(STM32F101xG) || defined(STM32F103xE) || defined(STM32F103xG)
/**
  * @brief  Setup the external memory controller. Called in startup_stm32f1xx.s 
  *          before jump to __main
  * @param  None
  * @retval None
  */ 
#ifdef DATA_IN_ExtSRAM
/**
  * @brief  Setup the external memory controller. 
  *         Called in startup_stm32f1xx_xx.s/.c before jump to main.
  *         This function configures the external SRAM mounted on STM3210E-EVAL
  *         board (STM32 High density devices). This SRAM will be used as program
  *         data memory (including heap and stack).
  * @param  None
  * @retval None
  */ 
void SystemInit_ExtMemCtl(void) 
{
  __IO uint32_t tmpreg;
  /*!< FSMC Bank1 NOR/SRAM3 is used for the STM3210E-EVAL, if another Bank is 
    required, then adjust the Register Addresses */

  /* Enable FSMC clock */
  RCC->AHBENR = 0x00000114U;

  /* Delay after an RCC peripheral clock enabling */
  tmpreg = READ_BIT(RCC->AHBENR, RCC_AHBENR_FSMCEN);
  
  /* Enable GPIOD, GPIOE, GPIOF and GPIOG clocks */
  RCC->APB2ENR = 0x000001E0U;
  
  /* Delay after an RCC peripheral clock enabling */
  tmpreg = READ_BIT(RCC->APB2ENR, RCC_APB2ENR_IOPDEN);

  (void)(tmpreg);
  
/* ---------------  SRAM Data lines, NOE and NWE configuration ---------------*/
/*----------------  SRAM Address lines configuration -------------------------*/
/*----------------  NOE and NWE configuration --------------------------------*/  
/*----------------  NE3 configuration ----------------------------------------*/
/*----------------  NBL0, NBL1 configuration ---------------------------------*/
  
  GPIOD->CRL = 0x44BB44BBU;  
  GPIOD->CRH = 0xBBBBBBBBU;

  GPIOE->CRL = 0xB44444BBU;  
  GPIOE->CRH = 0xBBBBBBBBU;

  GPIOF->CRL = 0x44BBBBBBU;  
  GPIOF->CRH = 0xBBBB4444U;

  GPIOG->CRL = 0x44BBBBBBU;  
  GPIOG->CRH = 0x444B4B44U;
   
/*----------------  FSMC Configuration ---------------------------------------*/  
/*----------------  Enable FSMC Bank1_SRAM Bank ------------------------------*/
  
  FSMC_Bank1->BTCR[4U] = 0x00001091U;
  FSMC_Bank1->BTCR[5U] = 0x00110212U;
}
#endif /* DATA_IN_ExtSRAM */
#endif /* STM32F100xE || STM32F101xE || STM32F101xG || STM32F103xE || STM32F103xG */

/**
  * @}
  */

/**
  * @}
  */
  
/**
  * @}

/**
 ******************************************************************************
 * @file      sysmem.c
 * @author    Generated by STM32CubeIDE
 * @brief     STM32CubeIDE System Memory calls file
 *
 *            For more information about which C functions
 *            need which of these lowlevel functions
 *            please consult the newlib libc manual
 ******************************************************************************
 * @attention
 *
 * Copyright (c) 2026 STMicroelectronics.
 * All rights reserved.
 *
 * This software is licensed under terms that can be found in the LICENSE file
 * in the root directory of this software component.
 * If no LICENSE file comes with this software, it is provided AS-IS.
 *
 ******************************************************************************
 */

/* Includes */
#include <errno.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

/**
 * Pointer to the current high watermark of the heap usage
 */
static uint8_t *__sbrk_heap_end = NULL;

/**
 * @brief _sbrk() allocates memory to the newlib heap and is used by malloc
 *        and others from the C library
 *
 * @verbatim
 * ############################################################################
 * #  .data  #  .bss  #       newlib heap       #          MSP stack          #
 * #         #        #                         # Reserved by _Min_Stack_Size #
 * ############################################################################
 * ^-- RAM start      ^-- _end                             _estack, RAM end --^
 * @endverbatim
 *
 * This implementation starts allocating at the '_end' linker symbol
 * The '_Min_Stack_Size' linker symbol reserves a memory for the MSP stack
 * The implementation considers '_estack' linker symbol to be RAM end
 * NOTE: If the MSP stack, at any point during execution, grows larger than the
 * reserved size, please increase the '_Min_Stack_Size'.
 *
 * @param incr Memory size
 * @return Pointer to allocated memory
 */
void *_sbrk(ptrdiff_t incr)
{
  extern uint8_t _end; /* Symbol defined in the linker script */
  extern uint8_t _estack; /* Symbol defined in the linker script */
  extern uint32_t _Min_Stack_Size; /* Symbol defined in the linker script */
  const uint32_t stack_limit = (uint32_t)&_estack - (uint32_t)&_Min_Stack_Size;
  const uint8_t *max_heap = (uint8_t *)stack_limit;
  uint8_t *prev_heap_end;

  /* Initialize heap end at first call */
  if (NULL == __sbrk_heap_end)
  {
    __sbrk_heap_end = &_end;
  }

  /* Protect heap from growing into the reserved MSP stack */
  if (__sbrk_heap_end + incr > max_heap)
  {
    errno = ENOMEM;
    return (void *)-1;
  }

  prev_heap_end = __sbrk_heap_end;
  __sbrk_heap_end += incr;

  return (void *)prev_heap_end;
}
#if defined(__PICOLIBC__)
  // Picolibc expects syscalls without the leading underscore.
  // This creates a strong alias so that
  // calls to `sbrk()` are resolved to our `_sbrk()` implementation.
  __strong_reference(_sbrk, sbrk);
#endif

/**

  * @param  cmd: Command code
  * @param  pbuf: Buffer containing command data (request parameters)
  * @param  length: Number of data to be sent (in bytes)
  * @retval Result of the operation: USBD_OK if all operations are OK else USBD_FAIL
  */
static int8_t CDC_Control_FS(uint8_t cmd, uint8_t* pbuf, uint16_t length)
{
  /* USER CODE BEGIN 5 */
  switch(cmd)
  {
    case CDC_SEND_ENCAPSULATED_COMMAND:

    break;

    case CDC_GET_ENCAPSULATED_RESPONSE:

    break;

    case CDC_SET_COMM_FEATURE:

    break;

    case CDC_GET_COMM_FEATURE:

    break;

    case CDC_CLEAR_COMM_FEATURE:

    break;

  /*******************************************************************************/
  /* Line Coding Structure                                                       */
  /*-----------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
  /* Offset | Field       | Size | Value  | Description                          */
  /* 0      | dwDTERate   |   4  | Number |Data terminal rate, in bits per second*/
  /* 4      | bCharFormat |   1  | Number | Stop bits                            */
  /*                                        0 - 1 Stop bit                       */
  /*                                        1 - 1.5 Stop bits                    */
  /*                                        2 - 2 Stop bits                      */
  /* 5      | bParityType |  1   | Number | Parity                               */
  /*                                        0 - None                             */
  /*                                        1 - Odd                              */
  /*                                        2 - Even                             */
  /*                                        3 - Mark                             */
  /*                                        4 - Space                            */
  /* 6      | bDataBits  |   1   | Number Data bits (5, 6, 7, 8 or 16).          */
  /*******************************************************************************/
    case CDC_SET_LINE_CODING:

    break;

    case CDC_GET_LINE_CODING:

    break;

    case CDC_SET_CONTROL_LINE_STATE:

    break;

    case CDC_SEND_BREAK:

    break;

  default:
    break;
  }

  return (USBD_OK);
  /* USER CODE END 5 */
}

/**
  * @brief  Data received over USB OUT endpoint are sent over CDC interface
  *         through this function.
  *
  *         @note
  *         This function will issue a NAK packet on any OUT packet received on
  *         USB endpoint until exiting this function. If you exit this function
  *         before transfer is complete on CDC interface (ie. using DMA controller)
  *         it will result in receiving more data while previous ones are still
  *         not sent.
  *
  * @param  Buf: Buffer of data to be received
  * @param  Len: Number of data received (in bytes)
  * @retval Result of the operation: USBD_OK if all operations are OK else USBD_FAIL
  */
static int8_t CDC_Receive_FS(uint8_t* Buf, uint32_t *Len)
{
  /* USER CODE BEGIN 6 */
  /* Прерывание должно быть коротким: разбираем команду и сразу
     возвращаем приёмник в работу. Никаких стираний Flash здесь. */
  if (*Len > 0) mc_cmd_parse(Buf, *Len);

  USBD_CDC_SetRxBuffer(&hUsbDeviceFS, &Buf[0]);
  USBD_CDC_ReceivePacket(&hUsbDeviceFS);
  return (USBD_OK);
  /* USER CODE END 6 */
}

/**
  * @brief  CDC_Transmit_FS
  *         Data to send over USB IN endpoint are sent over CDC interface
  *         through this function.
  *         @note
  *
  *
  * @param  Buf: Buffer of data to be sent
  * @param  Len: Number of data to be sent (in bytes)
  * @retval USBD_OK if all operations are OK else USBD_FAIL or USBD_BUSY
  */
uint8_t CDC_Transmit_FS(uint8_t* Buf, uint16_t Len)
{
  uint8_t result = USBD_OK;
  /* USER CODE BEGIN 7 */
  USBD_CDC_HandleTypeDef *hcdc = (USBD_CDC_HandleTypeDef*)hUsbDeviceFS.pClassData;
  if (hcdc == NULL) return USBD_FAIL;          /* кабель не подключён */
  if (hcdc->TxState != 0){
    return USBD_BUSY;
  }
  USBD_CDC_SetTxBuffer(&hUsbDeviceFS, Buf, Len);
  result = USBD_CDC_TransmitPacket(&hUsbDeviceFS);
  /* USER CODE END 7 */
  return result;
}

/* USER CODE END PRIVATE_FUNCTIONS_IMPLEMENTATION */

  */

/**
  * @}
  */

  else

}

   * ID */

  /* USER CODE BEGIN USBD_FS_SerialStrDescriptor */

  return (uint8_t *) USBD_StringSerial;
}

  }

  }
  return USBD_StrDesc;
}

/**
  * @brief  Create the serial number string descriptor
  * @param  None
  * @retval None
  */
static void Get_SerialNum(void)
{
  uint32_t deviceserial0;
  uint32_t deviceserial1;
  uint32_t deviceserial2;

  deviceserial0 = *(uint32_t *) DEVICE_ID1;
  deviceserial1 = *(uint32_t *) DEVICE_ID2;
  deviceserial2 = *(uint32_t *) DEVICE_ID3;

  deviceserial0 += deviceserial2;

  if (deviceserial0 != 0)
  {
    IntToUnicode(deviceserial0, &USBD_StringSerial[2], 8);
    IntToUnicode(deviceserial1, &USBD_StringSerial[18], 4);
  }
}

/**
  * @brief  Convert Hex 32Bits value into char
  * @param  value: value to convert
  * @param  pbuf: pointer to the buffer
  * @param  len: buffer length
  * @retval None
  */
static void IntToUnicode(uint32_t value, uint8_t * pbuf, uint8_t len)
{
  uint8_t idx = 0;

  for (idx = 0; idx < len; idx++)
  {
    if (((value >> 28)) < 0xA)
    {
      pbuf[2 * idx] = (value >> 28) + '0';
    }
    else
    {
      pbuf[2 * idx] = (value >> 28) + 'A' - 10;
    }

    value = value << 4;

    pbuf[2 * idx + 1] = 0;
  }
}

/**
  * @}

  * @param  hpcd: PCD handle
  * @retval None
  */
#if (USE_HAL_PCD_REGISTER_CALLBACKS == 1U)
static void PCD_SOFCallback(PCD_HandleTypeDef *hpcd)
#else
void HAL_PCD_SOFCallback(PCD_HandleTypeDef *hpcd)
#endif /* USE_HAL_PCD_REGISTER_CALLBACKS */
{
  USBD_LL_SOF((USBD_HandleTypeDef*)hpcd->pData);
}

/**
  * @brief  Reset callback.
  * @param  hpcd: PCD handle
  * @retval None
  */
#if (USE_HAL_PCD_REGISTER_CALLBACKS == 1U)
static void PCD_ResetCallback(PCD_HandleTypeDef *hpcd)
#else
void HAL_PCD_ResetCallback(PCD_HandleTypeDef *hpcd)
#endif /* USE_HAL_PCD_REGISTER_CALLBACKS */
{
  USBD_SpeedTypeDef speed = USBD_SPEED_FULL;

  if ( hpcd->Init.speed != PCD_SPEED_FULL)
  {
    Error_Handler();
  }
    /* Set Speed. */
  USBD_LL_SetSpeed((USBD_HandleTypeDef*)hpcd->pData, speed);

  /* Reset Device. */
  USBD_LL_Reset((USBD_HandleTypeDef*)hpcd->pData);
}

/**
  * @brief  Suspend callback.
  * When Low power mode is enabled the debug cannot be used (IAR, Keil doesn't support it)
  * @param  hpcd: PCD handle
  * @retval None
  */
#if (USE_HAL_PCD_REGISTER_CALLBACKS == 1U)
static void PCD_SuspendCallback(PCD_HandleTypeDef *hpcd)
#else
void HAL_PCD_SuspendCallback(PCD_HandleTypeDef *hpcd)
#endif /* USE_HAL_PCD_REGISTER_CALLBACKS */
{
  /* Inform USB library that core enters in suspend Mode. */
  USBD_LL_Suspend((USBD_HandleTypeDef*)hpcd->pData);
  /* Enter in STOP mode. */
  /* USER CODE BEGIN 2 */
  if (hpcd->Init.low_power_enable)
  {
    /* Set SLEEPDEEP bit and SleepOnExit of Cortex System Control Register. */
    SCB->SCR |= (uint32_t)((uint32_t)(SCB_SCR_SLEEPDEEP_Msk | SCB_SCR_SLEEPONEXIT_Msk));
  }
  /* USER CODE END 2 */
}

/**
  * @brief  Resume callback.
  * When Low power mode is enabled the debug cannot be used (IAR, Keil doesn't support it)
  * @param  hpcd: PCD handle
  * @retval None
  */
#if (USE_HAL_PCD_REGISTER_CALLBACKS == 1U)
static void PCD_ResumeCallback(PCD_HandleTypeDef *hpcd)
#else
void HAL_PCD_ResumeCallback(PCD_HandleTypeDef *hpcd)
#endif /* USE_HAL_PCD_REGISTER_CALLBACKS */
{
  /* USER CODE BEGIN 3 */

  /* USER CODE END 3 */
  USBD_LL_Resume((USBD_HandleTypeDef*)hpcd->pData);
}

/**
  * @brief  ISOOUTIncomplete callback.
  * @param  hpcd: PCD handle
  * @param  epnum: Endpoint number
  * @retval None
  */
#if (USE_HAL_PCD_REGISTER_CALLBACKS == 1U)
static void PCD_ISOOUTIncompleteCallback(PCD_HandleTypeDef *hpcd, uint8_t epnum)
#else
void HAL_PCD_ISOOUTIncompleteCallback(PCD_HandleTypeDef *hpcd, uint8_t epnum)
#endif /* USE_HAL_PCD_REGISTER_CALLBACKS */

  USBD_LL_IsoOUTIncomplete((USBD_HandleTypeDef*)hpcd->pData, epnum);

  * @retval None
  */
#if (USE_HAL_PCD_REGISTER_CALLBACKS == 1U)

#else

  */
#if (USE_HAL_PCD_REGISTER_CALLBACKS == 1U)

/*******************************************************************************
                       LL Driver Interface (USB Device Library --> PCD)
*******************************************************************************/

/**
  * @brief  Initializes the low level portion of the device driver.
  * @param  pdev: Device handle
  * @retval USBD status
  */
USBD_StatusTypeDef USBD_LL_Init(USBD_HandleTypeDef *pdev)
{
  /* Init USB Ip. */
  /* Link the driver to the stack. */
  hpcd_USB_FS.pData = pdev;
  pdev->pData = &hpcd_USB_FS;

  hpcd_USB_FS.Instance = USB;
  hpcd_USB_FS.Init.dev_endpoints = 8;
  hpcd_USB_FS.Init.speed = PCD_SPEED_FULL;
  hpcd_USB_FS.Init.low_power_enable = DISABLE;
  hpcd_USB_FS.Init.lpm_enable = DISABLE;
  hpcd_USB_FS.Init.battery_charging_enable = DISABLE;
  if (HAL_PCD_Init(&hpcd_USB_FS) != HAL_OK)
  {
    Error_Handler( );
  }

#if (USE_HAL_PCD_REGISTER_CALLBACKS == 1U)
  /* Register USB PCD CallBacks */
  HAL_PCD_RegisterCallback(&hpcd_USB_FS, HAL_PCD_SOF_CB_ID, PCD_SOFCallback);
  HAL_PCD_RegisterCallback(&hpcd_USB_FS, HAL_PCD_SETUPSTAGE_CB_ID, PCD_SetupStageCallback);
  HAL_PCD_RegisterCallback(&hpcd_USB_FS, HAL_PCD_RESET_CB_ID, PCD_ResetCallback);
  HAL_PCD_RegisterCallback(&hpcd_USB_FS, HAL_PCD_SUSPEND_CB_ID, PCD_SuspendCallback);
  HAL_PCD_RegisterCallback(&hpcd_USB_FS, HAL_PCD_RESUME_CB_ID, PCD_ResumeCallback);
  HAL_PCD_RegisterCallback(&hpcd_USB_FS, HAL_PCD_CONNECT_CB_ID, PCD_ConnectCallback);
  HAL_PCD_RegisterCallback(&hpcd_USB_FS, HAL_PCD_DISCONNECT_CB_ID, PCD_DisconnectCallback);

  HAL_PCD_RegisterDataOutStageCallback(&hpcd_USB_FS, PCD_DataOutStageCallback);
  HAL_PCD_RegisterDataInStageCallback(&hpcd_USB_FS, PCD_DataInStageCallback);
  HAL_PCD_RegisterIsoOutIncpltCallback(&hpcd_USB_FS, PCD_ISOOUTIncompleteCallback);
  HAL_PCD_RegisterIsoInIncpltCallback(&hpcd_USB_FS, PCD_ISOINIncompleteCallback);
#endif /* USE_HAL_PCD_REGISTER_CALLBACKS */
  /* USER CODE BEGIN EndPoint_Configuration */
  HAL_PCDEx_PMAConfig((PCD_HandleTypeDef*)pdev->pData , 0x00 , PCD_SNG_BUF, 0x18);
  HAL_PCDEx_PMAConfig((PCD_HandleTypeDef*)pdev->pData , 0x80 , PCD_SNG_BUF, 0x58);
  /* USER CODE END EndPoint_Configuration */
  /* USER CODE BEGIN EndPoint_Configuration_CDC */
  HAL_PCDEx_PMAConfig((PCD_HandleTypeDef*)pdev->pData , 0x81 , PCD_SNG_BUF, 0xC0);
  HAL_PCDEx_PMAConfig((PCD_HandleTypeDef*)pdev->pData , 0x01 , PCD_SNG_BUF, 0x110);
  HAL_PCDEx_PMAConfig((PCD_HandleTypeDef*)pdev->pData , 0x82 , PCD_SNG_BUF, 0x100);
  /* USER CODE END EndPoint_Configuration_CDC */
  return USBD_OK;
}

  * @brief  De-Initializes the low level portion of the device driver.

USBD_StatusTypeDef USBD_LL_DeInit(USBD_HandleTypeDef *pdev)
{
  HAL_StatusTypeDef hal_status = HAL_OK;
  USBD_StatusTypeDef usb_status = USBD_OK;

  hal_status = HAL_PCD_DeInit(pdev->pData);

  usb_status =  USBD_Get_USB_Status(hal_status);

  return usb_status;
}

/**
  * @brief  Returns Stall condition.
  * @param  pdev: Device handle
  * @param  ep_addr: Endpoint number
  * @retval Stall (1: Yes, 0: No)
  */
uint8_t USBD_LL_IsStallEP(USBD_HandleTypeDef *pdev, uint8_t ep_addr)
{
  PCD_HandleTypeDef *hpcd = (PCD_HandleTypeDef*) pdev->pData;

  if((ep_addr & 0x80) == 0x80)
  {
    return hpcd->IN_ep[ep_addr & 0x7F].is_stall;
  }
  else
  {
    return hpcd->OUT_ep[ep_addr & 0x7F].is_stall;
  }
}

/**
  * @brief Software Device Connection
  * @param hpcd: PCD handle

  * @retval None

#else

#endif /* USE_HAL_PCD_REGISTER_CALLBACKS */
{
  /* USER CODE BEGIN 6 */
  if (state == 1)
  {
    /* Configure Low connection state. */

  }
  else
  {
    /* Configure High connection state. */

  }
  /* USER CODE END 6 */
}

/**
  * @brief  Returns the USB status depending on the HAL status:
  * @param  hal_status: HAL status
  * @retval USB status
  */
USBD_StatusTypeDef USBD_Get_USB_Status(HAL_StatusTypeDef hal_status)
{
  USBD_StatusTypeDef usb_status = USBD_OK;

  switch (hal_status)
  {
    case HAL_OK :
      usb_status = USBD_OK;
    break;
    case HAL_ERROR :
      usb_status = USBD_FAIL;
    break;
    case HAL_BUSY :
      usb_status = USBD_BUSY;
    break;
    case HAL_TIMEOUT :
      usb_status = USBD_FAIL;
    break;
    default :
      usb_status = USBD_FAIL;
    break;
  }
  return usb_status;
}

/**
  *  Беспроводной COM-порт для STM32 телеметрии через ESP8266 (D1 mini).
  *
  *  Заливается в Arduino IDE (плата Lolin(Wemos) D1 mini).
  *  Роль: прозрачный TCP-сервер на порту 23. Всё, что STM32 шлёт по
  *  USART3, уходит клиенту (ПК/смартфон) и наоборот.
  *
  *  СВЯЗЬ С STM32:
  *    D1 mini GPIO13(RX) <- STM32 PB10(USART3_TX)
  *    D1 mini GPIO15(TX) -> STM32 PB11(USART3_RX)
  *    сделать Serial.swap() — ОБЯЗАТЕЛЬНО, см. ниже.
  *
  *  ПИТАНИЕ: +5V на пин 5V модуля (у него СВОЙ регулятор, НЕ вешать на 3.3V
  *  Blue Pill — там регулятор уже отдаёт ток на МК и USB). C 470 мкФ у модуля
  *  обязателен. +5V источник ~700 мА.
  */

#include <ESP8266WiFi.h>

const char* SSID = "ваша_сеть";
const char* PASS = "пароль";
const uint32_t BAUD = 460800;          // должна совпадать с WIFI_BAUD в mc_wifi.h

WiFiServer server(23);
WiFiClient client;

void setup() {
  Serial.begin(BAUD);
  // ОБЯЗАТЕЛЬНО. Штатные пины UART0 (D9/RX, D10/TX) заняты загрузчиком и
  // отладочным выводом: при старте туда сыплется мусор на 74880 бод,
  // который пошёл бы в STM32. swap() переводит аппаратный UART0 на
  // GPIO13(RX) / GPIO15(TX), а загрузочный мусор остаётся на штатных.
  Serial.swap();

  WiFi.mode(WIFI_STA);
  WiFi.begin(SSID, PASS);
  while (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED) delay(200);

  server.begin();
  server.setNoDelay(true);   // без этого Nagle склеит кадры и добавит 40 мс
}

void loop() {
  if (server.hasClient()) {
    if (!client || !client.connected()) client = server.available();
    else server.available().stop();     // один клиент за раз
  }

  // UART -> Wi-Fi (телеметрия)
  static uint8_t buf[1024];
  int n = (client && client.connected()) ? Serial.available() : 0;
  if (n > 0) {
    if (n > (int)sizeof(buf)) n = sizeof(buf);
    Serial.readBytes(buf, n);
    client.write(buf, n);
  }

  // Wi-Fi -> UART (команды управления от ПК/телефона)
  while (client && client.available()) Serial.write(client.read());
}

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
mc_config — настройка контроллера двигателя без перепрошивки.
Читает текущие значения из телеметрии, пишет команды, сохраняет во Flash ('w').
"""
import serial, serial.tools.list_ports, tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox
import threading, re, json, time

BAUD = 115200

PARAMS = [
    ('kp',   'KP', 'Kp — контур скорости',      0, 5000, 'жёсткость удержания оборотов'),
    ('ki',   'KI', 'Ki — контур скорости',      0, 5000, 'устраняет статическую ошибку'),
    ('kd',   'KD', 'Kd — контур скорости',      0, 5000, 'гасит раскачку'),
    ('cikp', 'CP', 'Kp — контур тока',          1, 5000, 'форма пускового тока'),
    ('ciki', 'CI', 'Ki — контур тока',          0, 5000, 'форма пускового тока'),
    ('cl',   'CL', 'Предел тока, отсчёты АЦП', 50, 3000, '763 = 1.00 А, 1500 ≈ 2.0 А'),
    ('ss',   'SS', 'SS — нарастание ШИМ',       1,  100, 'цифровой конденсатор'),
    ('ru',   'RU', 'RU — разгон уставки',       1,  100, 'об/мин за шаг'),
    ('rd',   'RD', 'RD — торможение уставки',   1,  100, 'об/мин за шаг'),
    ('uv',   'UV', 'Напряжение сети, В',      100,  300, 'для пересчёта на двигателе'),
]

# Параметры без обратного чтения (нет в JSON, но можно писать)
WRITE_ONLY = [
    ('pause','P',  'Пауза после защиты, мс',  100,30000, 'авторестарт по перегрузке'),
]

FLAGS = [
    ('te', 'TE', 'Тепловая защита I²t'),
    ('me', 'ME', 'Защита по пику тока'),
    ('tl', 'TL', 'Ограничение момента'),
]

MODES = [('0', 'Рабочий'), ('1', 'Отладка'), ('2', 'Тест'), ('3', 'Тяговый')]


class ShuntCalc(tk.Frame):
    def __init__(self, master, on_apply):
        super().__init__(master, padx=10, pady=8)
        self.on_apply = on_apply
        tk.Label(self, text='Калькулятор предела тока',
                 font=('Arial', 11, 'bold')).grid(row=0, column=0, columnspan=3, sticky='w')
        self.e_r = self._row(1, 'Шунт, Ом:', '0.075')
        self.e_g = self._row(2, 'Усиление датчика:', '8.2')
        self.e_i = self._row(3, 'Желаемый предел, А:', '1.0')
        tk.Button(self, text='Посчитать', command=self.calc).grid(row=4, column=0, pady=6)
        self.out = tk.Label(self, text='', fg='#166534', font=('Consolas', 10), justify='left')
        self.out.grid(row=4, column=1, columnspan=2, sticky='w')
        tk.Button(self, text='Подставить в «Предел тока»',
                  command=self.apply).grid(row=5, column=0, columnspan=3, pady=4)
        self.adc = None
        self.calc()

    def _row(self, r, label, default):
        tk.Label(self, text=label).grid(row=r, column=0, sticky='w', pady=2)
        e = tk.Entry(self, width=12)
        e.insert(0, default)
        e.grid(row=r, column=1, sticky='w')
        e.bind('<Return>', lambda ev: self.calc())
        return e

    def calc(self):
        try:
            r = float(self.e_r.get()); g = float(self.e_g.get()); i = float(self.e_i.get())
            mv = r * i * 1000.0
            adc = int(round(4095.0 * (r * i * g) / 3.3))
            adc = max(0, min(4095, adc))
            self.adc = adc
            warn = ''
            if mv > 300:   warn = '\n⚠ >300 мВ — абсолютный максимум датчика!'
            elif mv > 250: warn = '\n⚠ >250 мВ — вне линейного диапазона'
            elif mv > 200: warn = '\n⚠ близко к границе линейности'
            self.out.config(text=f'На шунте {mv:.0f} мВ → АЦП {adc}{warn}',
                            fg='#b91c1c' if warn else '#166534')
        except Exception:
            self.out.config(text='Введи числа', fg='#b91c1c')

    def apply(self):
        if self.adc:
            self.on_apply('cl', self.adc)


class App:
    def __init__(self, root):
        self.root = root
        root.title('Настройки контроллера двигателя')
        root.geometry('720x760')
        self.ser = None
        self.running = False
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self.raw = b''
        self.live = {}
        self.zones = None
        self.zvars = []
        self.vars = {}
        self.flag_vars = {}
        self.mode_var = tk.StringVar(value='0')
        self.dirty = False
        root.protocol('WM_DELETE_WINDOW', self.close)
        self.build_connect()

    def build_connect(self):
        self.fr_conn = tk.Frame(self.root)
        self.fr_conn.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)
        tk.Label(self.fr_conn, text='Выбери COM-порт контроллера:',
                 font=('Arial', 13)).pack(pady=20)
        self.ports = list(serial.tools.list_ports.comports())
        if not self.ports:
            tk.Label(self.fr_conn, text='Портов не найдено', fg='red').pack()
            return
        self.lb = tk.Listbox(self.fr_conn, height=8, font=('Consolas', 10))
        for p in self.ports:
            self.lb.insert(tk.END, f'{p.device} — {p.description}')
        self.lb.pack(fill=tk.X, padx=40)
        self.lb.selection_set(0)
        tk.Button(self.fr_conn, text='Подключить', command=self.connect,
                  bg='#2563eb', fg='white', padx=30, pady=6).pack(pady=16)

    def connect(self):
        sel = self.lb.curselection()
        if not sel: return
        try:
            self.ser = serial.Serial(self.ports[sel[0]].device, BAUD, timeout=0.3)
            time.sleep(0.4)
            self.ser.reset_input_buffer()
            self.ser.write(b'j')
            self.running = True
            threading.Thread(target=self.read_loop, daemon=True).start()
            self.fr_conn.destroy()
            self.build_main()
            self.root.after(600, self.pull)
        except Exception as e:
            messagebox.showerror('Ошибка', str(e))

    def read_loop(self):
        while self.running:
            try:
                n = self.ser.in_waiting
                if n == 0:
                    time.sleep(0.005)
                    continue
                data = self.ser.read(n)
                with self.lock:
                    self.raw += data
                    if len(self.raw) > 8192:
                        self.raw = self.raw[-4096:]
                    while True:
                        m = re.search(rb'\{[^{}]+\}', self.raw)
                        if not m: break
                        chunk = m.group(0)
                        self.raw = self.raw[m.end():]
                        try:
                            o = json.loads(chunk.decode('utf-8', errors='ignore'))
                            if 'zones' in o:
                                self.zones = o['zones']
                            else:
                                self.live = dict(o)
                        except Exception: pass
            except Exception: pass

    def build_main(self):
        nb = ttk.Notebook(self.root)
        nb.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True, padx=6, pady=6)
        tab1 = tk.Frame(nb); nb.add(tab1, text='Параметры')
        tab2 = tk.Frame(nb); nb.add(tab2, text='Защиты и режимы')
        tab3 = tk.Frame(nb); nb.add(tab3, text='Калькулятор шунта')
        tab4 = tk.Frame(nb); nb.add(tab4, text='Автонастройка (зоны)')
        self.build_zones(tab4)

        for i, (key, cmd, label, lo, hi, hint) in enumerate(PARAMS):
            tk.Label(tab1, text=label, anchor='w', font=('Arial', 10)) \
                .grid(row=i, column=0, sticky='w', padx=8, pady=3)
            v = tk.StringVar(value='—')
            self.vars[key] = v
            e = tk.Entry(tab1, textvariable=v, width=10, justify='right')
            e.grid(row=i, column=1, padx=4)
            e.bind('<Return>', lambda ev, k=key: self.send_one(k))
            tk.Button(tab1, text='→', width=3,
                      command=lambda k=key: self.send_one(k)).grid(row=i, column=2)
            tk.Label(tab1, text=f'{lo}…{hi}  {hint}', fg='#6b7280',
                     font=('Arial', 8)).grid(row=i, column=3, sticky='w', padx=6)

        tk.Button(tab1, text='Записать всё в контроллер', command=self.send_all,
                  bg='#2563eb', fg='white', padx=16, pady=5) \
            .grid(row=len(PARAMS), column=0, columnspan=2, pady=12)
        tk.Button(tab1, text='Считать с контроллера', command=self.pull,
                  padx=16, pady=5).grid(row=len(PARAMS), column=2, columnspan=2, pady=12)

        for i, (key, cmd, label) in enumerate(FLAGS):
            bv = tk.IntVar(value=1)
            self.flag_vars[key] = bv
            tk.Checkbutton(tab2, text=label, variable=bv, font=('Arial', 10),
                           command=lambda k=key: self.send_flag(k)) \
                .grid(row=i, column=0, sticky='w', padx=10, pady=4)

        tk.Label(tab2, text='Режим токовой защиты:', font=('Arial', 10, 'bold')) \
            .grid(row=10, column=0, sticky='w', padx=10, pady=(14, 2))
        for i, (code, name) in enumerate(MODES):
            tk.Radiobutton(tab2, text=f'{code} — {name}', variable=self.mode_var,
                           value=code, command=self.send_mode) \
                .grid(row=11 + i, column=0, sticky='w', padx=24)

        tk.Label(tab2, text='ПИД:', font=('Arial', 10, 'bold')) \
            .grid(row=20, column=0, sticky='w', padx=10, pady=(14, 2))
        fr = tk.Frame(tab2); fr.grid(row=21, column=0, sticky='w', padx=24)
        tk.Button(fr, text='Автоподстройка (a)', width=18,
                  command=lambda: self.cmd('a')).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=3)
        tk.Button(fr, text='Ручной (m)', width=12,
                  command=lambda: self.cmd('m')).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=3)

        fr2 = tk.Frame(tab2); fr2.grid(row=22, column=0, sticky='w', padx=24, pady=6)
        tk.Button(fr2, text='Компенсация пульсаций вкл (r)', width=28,
                  command=lambda: self.cmd('r')).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=3)
        tk.Button(fr2, text='выкл (s)', width=10,
                  command=lambda: self.cmd('s')).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=3)

        tk.Button(tab2, text='СБРОС ЗАЩИТЫ (RS)', command=lambda: self.cmd('RS'),
                  bg='#dc2626', fg='white', padx=16, pady=5) \
            .grid(row=30, column=0, sticky='w', padx=10, pady=18)

        ShuntCalc(tab3, self.set_field).pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)

        bottom = tk.Frame(self.root, bg='#111827')
        bottom.pack(fill=tk.X)
        tk.Button(bottom, text='Сохранить во Flash (w)', command=self.do_save,
                  bg='#16a34a', fg='white', padx=20, pady=5, font=('Arial', 11)) \
            .pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=10, pady=8)
        self.stat = tk.Label(bottom, text='...', bg='#111827', fg='#eab308',
                             font=('Consolas', 10), anchor='w')
        self.stat.pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=10, fill=tk.X, expand=True)

    ZONE_NAMES = ['Холостой ход', 'Низкие', 'Ниже средних',
                  'Средние', 'Высокие', 'Максимум']

    def build_zones(self, tab):
        tk.Label(tab, text='Коэффициенты по оборотам',
                 font=('Arial', 11, 'bold')).grid(row=0, column=0, columnspan=6,
                                                  sticky='w', padx=8, pady=(8, 2))
        tk.Label(tab, text='Между строками значения меняются плавно. Берётся по '
                           'ФАКТИЧЕСКИМ оборотам, работает только в режиме «Авто» (a).',
                 fg='#6b7280', font=('Arial', 8), justify='left') \
            .grid(row=1, column=0, columnspan=6, sticky='w', padx=8)

        for c, h in enumerate(['Зона', 'До скольких об/мин', 'Kp', 'Ki', 'Kd']):
            tk.Label(tab, text=h, font=('Arial', 9, 'bold')) \
                .grid(row=2, column=c, padx=4, pady=(8, 2))

        self.zvars = []
        for i in range(6):
            tk.Label(tab, text=f'{i}  {self.ZONE_NAMES[i]}', anchor='w',
                     font=('Arial', 9)).grid(row=3 + i, column=0, sticky='w',
                                             padx=8, pady=2)
            row = {}
            for c, key in enumerate(['rpm', 'kp', 'ki', 'kd']):
                v = tk.StringVar(value='—')
                e = tk.Entry(tab, textvariable=v, width=8, justify='right')
                e.grid(row=3 + i, column=1 + c, padx=4)
                e.bind('<Return>', lambda ev, n=i: self.send_zone(n))
                row[key] = v
            self.zvars.append(row)
            tk.Button(tab, text='→', width=3,
                      command=lambda n=i: self.send_zone(n)).grid(row=3 + i, column=5, padx=4)

        fr = tk.Frame(tab)
        fr.grid(row=10, column=0, columnspan=6, sticky='w', padx=8, pady=12)
        tk.Button(fr, text='Считать таблицу', command=self.pull_zones,
                  padx=12, pady=4).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=3)
        tk.Button(fr, text='Записать всю таблицу', command=self.send_zones_all,
                  bg='#2563eb', fg='white', padx=12, pady=4).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=3)
        tk.Button(fr, text='Заводские (ZR)', command=self.zones_default,
                  padx=12, pady=4).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=3)
        tk.Label(tab, text='После правки нажми «Сохранить во Flash» — иначе таблица '
                           'вернётся к прежней после перезагрузки.',
                 fg='#b45309', font=('Arial', 8)) \
            .grid(row=11, column=0, columnspan=6, sticky='w', padx=8)

    def pull_zones(self):
        with self.lock:
            self.zones = None
        self.cmd('ZD')
        self.root.after(500, self._fill_zones)

    def _fill_zones(self):
        with self.lock:
            z = self.zones
        if not z:
            self.status('Таблица не пришла — нужна прошивка с поддержкой ZD')
            return
        for i, row in enumerate(z[:6]):
            if len(row) < 4:
                continue
            for c, key in enumerate(['rpm', 'kp', 'ki', 'kd']):
                self.zvars[i][key].set(str(row[c]))
        self.status('Таблица считана')

    def send_zone(self, n):
        for key, L in [('kp', 'P'), ('ki', 'I'), ('kd', 'D'), ('rpm', 'R')]:
            raw = self.zvars[n][key].get().strip()
            if raw in ('', '—'):
                continue
            try:
                v = int(float(raw))
            except Exception:
                self.status(f'Зона {n}: нужно целое число')
                return
            lo, hi = (50, 12000) if key == 'rpm' else (0, 5000)
            if not (lo <= v <= hi):
                self.status(f'Зона {n}: {key} вне {lo}…{hi}')
                return
            self.cmd(f'Z{n}{L}{v}')
            time.sleep(0.05)
        self.dirty = True
        self.status(f'Зона {n} записана')

    def send_zones_all(self):
        for n in range(6):
            self.send_zone(n)
        self.status('Таблица записана')

    def zones_default(self):
        self.cmd('ZR')
        self.root.after(300, self.pull_zones)

    def cmd(self, c):
        try:
            if self.ser:
                self.ser.write(c.encode()); self.ser.flush()
        except Exception: pass

    def send_one(self, key):
        var = self.vars.get(key)
        if var is None:
            return
        for k, cmd, label, lo, hi, _ in PARAMS:
            if k != key:
                continue
            try:
                v = int(float(var.get()))
            except Exception:
                self.status(f'{label}: нужно целое число')
                return
            if not (lo <= v <= hi):
                self.status(f'{label}: допустимо {lo}…{hi}')
                return
            self.cmd(f'{cmd}{v}')
            self.dirty = True
            self.status(f'{label} = {v}')
            return

    def send_all(self):
        for key in self.vars:
            self.send_one(key)
            time.sleep(0.05)
        self.status('Записано')

    def send_flag(self, key):
        cmd_map = {'te':'TE','me':'ME','tl':'TL'}
        c = cmd_map.get(key)
        if not c: return
        v = self.flag_vars[key].get()
        self.ser.write(f'{c}{v}'.encode()); self.ser.flush()

    def send_mode(self):
        self.cmd(self.mode_var.get())

    def set_field(self, key, val):
        if key in self.vars:
            self.vars[key].set(str(val))

    def pull(self):
        time.sleep(0.3)
        kv = self.live
        if not kv:
            self.status('Нет данных: проверь кабель и режим j')
            return
        m = {'kp':'kp','ki':'ki','kd':'kd','cikp':'cikp','ciki':'ciki',
             'cl':'cls','ss':'ss','ru':'ru','rd':'rd','uv':'uv',
             'te':'te','me':'me','tl':'tl'}
        for k, vk in m.items():
            val = kv.get(vk, kv.get('cl' if vk == 'cls' else vk))
            if val is not None and k in self.vars:
                self.vars[k].set(str(val))
        if 'te' in kv: self.flag_vars['te'].set(kv['te'])
        if 'me' in kv: self.flag_vars['me'].set(kv['me'])
        if 'tl' in kv: self.flag_vars['tl'].set(kv['tl'])
        if 'prot' in kv:
            self.mode_var.set(str(kv['prot']))
        self.status('Считано')

    def do_save(self):
        if self.live.get('on', 0):
            self.status('Сначала выключи двигатель (on=0)!')
            return
        self.cmd('w')
        self.status('Сохранено во Flash')

    def status(self, t):
        self.stat.config(text=t)
        self.root.after(2000, lambda: self.stat.config(text='...'))

    def close(self):
        self.running = False
        try:
            if self.ser:
                self.ser.write(b'o')
                self.ser.close()
        except Exception: pass
        self.root.destroy()


def main():
    root = tk.Tk()
    App(root)
    root.mainloop()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
PID Tuner — контроллер коллекторного двигателя (Blue Pill F103).
- Графики оборотов и тока ВО ВЕСЬ ОСТАТОК окна (главный приоритет)
- Вкладки снизу фиксированной высоты: ПИД / Разгон / Разомкнутый / Фильтры /
  Зоны / Железо / Лог-Тест / Задатчик
- Крутилка виртуального задатчика + показ реального потенциометра
- Очередь команд (txq), отложенные ползунки, CSV-лог, тест скачка
- Одна компактная строка состояния

Формат прошивки (JSON): {"cur":...,"rpm":...,"duty":...,"ol":...,"olduty":...}
"""
import struct, serial, serial.tools.list_ports
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox
import threading, re, json, time, queue, csv, os, datetime, math

BAUD = 115200
RPM_MIN = 30
RPM_MAX = 6000
PWM_MAX = 900
DEFAULT_SS = 6
DEFAULT_RU = 10
DEFAULT_RD = 30
STEP_WINDOW = 5.0     # окно замера перерегулирования, с

TRIP_NAMES = {1:'перегрузка', 2:'пик тока', 3:'тепловая',
              4:'энкодер', 5:'разгон', 6:'стоянка в тяге',
              7:'датчик тока', 8:'обрыв якоря', 9:'нет связи',
              10:'сброс по watchdog', 11:'аппаратный срыв (BKIN)'}


class Knob(tk.Canvas):
    """Круглая ручка. Тянешь мышью по кругу или крутишь колесом — значение.
       -135°(минимум) .. +135°(максимум), разрыв дуги снизу."""

    def __init__(self, master, vmin, vmax, value, on_change, size=150, **kw):
        super().__init__(master, width=size, height=size,
                         bg='#101418', highlightthickness=0, **kw)
        self.vmin, self.vmax = vmin, vmax
        self.value = float(value)
        self.on_change = on_change
        self.size = size
        self.r = size / 2 - 14
        self.cx = self.cy = size / 2
        self._dragging = False
        self.bind('<Button-1>', self._press)
        self.bind('<B1-Motion>', self._drag)
        self.bind('<ButtonRelease-1>', lambda e: setattr(self, '_dragging', False))
        self.bind('<MouseWheel>', self._wheel)
        self.bind('<Button-4>', lambda e: self._wheel(e, 1))
        self.bind('<Button-5>', lambda e: self._wheel(e, -1))
        self.redraw()

    def _val2ang(self, v):
        f = (v - self.vmin) / float(self.vmax - self.vmin)
        f = max(0.0, min(1.0, f))
        return math.radians(-135 + 270 * f)

    def _press(self, ev):
        self._dragging = True
        self._drag(ev)

    def _drag(self, ev):
        dx, dy = ev.x - self.cx, ev.y - self.cy
        a = math.degrees(math.atan2(dy, dx)) + 90      # 0 = вверх
        if a > 180: a -= 360
        a = max(-135, min(135, a))                     # не даём перескочить
        f = (a + 135) / 270.0
        self.value = self.vmin + f * (self.vmax - self.vmin)
        self.redraw()
        self.on_change(self.value)

    def _wheel(self, ev, direction=None):
        step = (self.vmax - self.vmin) / 100.0
        d = direction if direction is not None else (1 if ev.delta > 0 else -1)
        self.value = max(self.vmin, min(self.vmax, self.value + d * step))
        self.redraw()
        self.on_change(self.value)

    def redraw(self):
        self.delete('all')
        cx, cy, r = self.cx, self.cy, self.r
        # шкала-дуга (серая, вся)
        self.create_arc(cx-r-6, cy-r-6, cx+r+6, cy+r+6,
                        start=-45, extent=-270, style=tk.ARC,
                        outline='#374151', width=3)
        # заполненная часть (зелёная)
        f = (self.value - self.vmin) / float(self.vmax - self.vmin)
        f = max(0.0, min(1.0, f))
        self.create_arc(cx-r-6, cy-r-6, cx+r+6, cy+r+6,
                        start=-45, extent=-270*f, style=tk.ARC,
                        outline='#22c55e', width=3)
        # корпус
        self.create_oval(cx-r, cy-r, cx+r, cy+r,
                         fill='#1f2937', outline='#4b5563', width=2)
        # риска
        a = self._val2ang(self.value)
        self.create_line(cx + r*0.35*math.sin(a), cy - r*0.35*math.cos(a),
                         cx + r*0.85*math.sin(a), cy - r*0.85*math.cos(a),
                         fill='#22c55e', width=4, capstyle=tk.ROUND)
        self.create_text(cx, cy+r*0.45, text=f'{int(self.value)}',
                         fill='#e5e7eb', font=('Consolas', 13, 'bold'))

    def set_value(self, v):
        """Обновление извне (телеметрия) — без обратного вызова."""
        self.value = max(self.vmin, min(self.vmax, float(v)))
        self.redraw()


class PidTuner:
    def __init__(self, root):
        self.root = root
        self.root.title('PID Tuner — контроллер двигателя')
        self.root.geometry('1180x840')
        self.root.protocol('WM_DELETE_WINDOW', self.on_close)
        self.ser = None
        self.running = False

        # --- Данные ---
        self.rpm_hist = [0.0] * 400
        self.cur_hist = [0.0] * 400
        self.raw = b''
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self.term_buf = []      # строки для терминала
        self.term_echo = False  # показывать ли поток телеметрии
        self.live = {}
        self.zones = None
        self.rpm_smooth = 0.0
        self.motor_v_smooth = 0.0
        self.power_smooth = 0.0     # сглаженная мощность, Вт
        self.power_w = 0.0
        self.u_dc_live = 325.0      # напряжение шины (после моста), В
        self.u_mot_live = 0.0       # напряжение на двигателе (ШИМ), В
        self.i_bus_live = 0.0       # ток шины (якорь × скважность), А
        self.ovr_prev = 0
        self.ovr_rate = 0
        self.ovr_tick = 0
        self.csv_file = None
        self.csv_writer = None
        self.logging = False

        # --- Очередь команд ---
        self.txq = queue.Queue()
        self._pid_job = None
        self._ramp_job = None
        self._ol_job = None
        self._filt_job = None
        self._ci_job = None
        self._rv_job = None
        self._rlim_job = None
        self._pot_anchor = None     # положение резистора на момент ухода на ПК
        self.last_frame = 0.0
        self.step_t0 = 0.0          # замер перерегулирования: 0 = не идёт
        self.step_to = 0
        self.step_from = 0
        self.step_peak = 0.0

        # --- Калибровка тока ---
        self.shunt_r = 0.075
        self.gain = 8.2

        self.panel_hidden = False
        self.dirty = False          # есть несохранённые во Flash настройки

        # --- Экран выбора порта ---
        self.port_frame = tk.Frame(root)
        self.port_frame.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)
        self.show_ports()

    @property
    def adc_to_a(self):
        return 3.3 / (4095.0 * self.shunt_r * self.gain)

    # ================= Выбор порта =================
    def show_ports(self):
        ports = list(serial.tools.list_ports.comports())
        tk.Label(self.port_frame, text='Выбери COM-порт контроллера:',
                 font=('Arial', 14)).pack(pady=20)
        if not ports:
            tk.Label(self.port_frame, text='Нет COM-портов', fg='red').pack()
            tk.Button(self.port_frame, text='Выход', command=self.root.destroy).pack()
            return
        self.listbox = tk.Listbox(self.port_frame, height=7, font=('Consolas', 11))
        for p in ports:
            self.listbox.insert(tk.END, f'{p.device} - {p.description}')
        self.listbox.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True, padx=40, pady=5)
        tk.Button(self.port_frame, text='Подключить',
                  command=lambda: self.connect(ports),
                  bg='#3b82f6', fg='white', padx=30, pady=6).pack(pady=20)

    def connect(self, ports):
        idx = self.listbox.curselection()
        if not idx:
            return
        try:
            self.ser = serial.Serial(ports[idx[0]].device, BAUD, timeout=0.3)
            time.sleep(0.4)
            self.ser.reset_input_buffer()
            self.ser.write(b'j')
            time.sleep(0.1)
            self.port_frame.pack_forget()
            self.running = True
            threading.Thread(target=self.tx_loop, daemon=True).start()
            threading.Thread(target=self.read_loop, daemon=True).start()
            self.start_ui()
            self.root.after(1000, self.send_heartbeat)
        except Exception as e:
            err = tk.Label(self.port_frame, text=f'Ошибка: {e}', fg='red')
            err.pack(pady=5)
            self.root.after(3000, err.destroy)

    def send_heartbeat(self):
        """Признак живой связи для прошивки. Телеметрию она отдаёт сама,
        без запросов, поэтому иначе TRIP_COMM срабатывает на исправном
        кабеле — просто потому, что человек ничего не трогает."""
        try:
            if self.ser is not None and self.ser.is_open:
                self.send_cmd('PG')
        except Exception:
            pass
        self.root.after(1000, self.send_heartbeat)

    # ================= Очередь команд =================
    def send_cmd(self, cmd):
        self.txq.put(cmd)
        # Команды-настройки (не пуск/стоп, не RV/RP) — помечаем несохранёнными.
        # RP/RV — источник уставки, не настройка; он же сам ставит sp_src.
        if hasattr(self, 'btn_save') and cmd:
            if cmd.startswith(('RV', 'RP', 'RS')):
                pass                        # источник оборотов / сброс — не в Flash
            elif cmd[0] in 'CDEFIKPRSTUV':
                self.dirty = True
                self.btn_save.config(text='Сохранить во Flash •', bg='#f97316')

    def tx_loop(self):
        while self.running:
            try:
                c = self.txq.get(timeout=0.2)
            except queue.Empty:
                continue
            try:
                self.ser.write((c + '\n').encode())
                self.ser.flush()
            except Exception:
                pass
            time.sleep(0.035)

    # ================= Отложенные ползунки =================
    def schedule_pid(self):
        if self._pid_job:
            self.root.after_cancel(self._pid_job)
        self._pid_job = self.root.after(150, self.send_pid)

    def schedule_ramp(self):
        if self._ramp_job:
            self.root.after_cancel(self._ramp_job)
        self._ramp_job = self.root.after(150, self.send_ramp)

    def schedule_ol(self):
        if self._ol_job:
            self.root.after_cancel(self._ol_job)
        self._ol_job = self.root.after(150, self.send_ol)

    def schedule_filt(self):
        if self._filt_job:
            self.root.after_cancel(self._filt_job)
        self._filt_job = self.root.after(150, self.send_filt)

    def schedule_ci(self):
        if self._ci_job:
            self.root.after_cancel(self._ci_job)
        self._ci_job = self.root.after(150, self.send_ci)

    def can_sync(self, var, hold=1.2):
        """Можно ли подтягивать ползунок из прошивки.
        Нельзя, пока он под мышью и ещё hold секунд после правки:
        в это окно приходят кадры со старым значением, и они откатят
        то, что человек только что выставил."""
        if getattr(var, '_drag', False):
            return False
        return (time.time() - getattr(var, '_touched', 0.0)) > hold

    def group_quiet(self, vars_, hold=1.5):
        """Группа ползунков «спокойна»: ни один не под мышью и ни один
        не правился последние hold секунд. Kp/Ki/Kd уходят в прошивку
        одной командой, поэтому и откатывать их можно только все вместе —
        иначе правка одного сбрасывает соседний."""
        now = time.time()
        for v in vars_:
            if getattr(v, '_drag', False):
                return False
            if (now - getattr(v, '_touched', 0.0)) <= hold:
                return False
        return True

    def pull_pid(self):
        """Разово подтянуть все ползунки ПИД под значения прошивки."""
        live = self.live or {}
        self.sync_var(self.var_kp, int(live.get('kp', 0)))
        self.sync_var(self.var_ki, int(live.get('ki', 0)))
        self.sync_var(self.var_kd, int(live.get('kd', 0)))
        self.sync_var(self.var_cikp, int(live.get('cikp', 0)))
        self.sync_var(self.var_ciki, int(live.get('ciki', 0)))

    def sync_group(self, pairs, job=None, hold=1.5):
        """Подтянуть группу ползунков из телеметрии.
        pairs: [(var, значение_из_прошивки), ...]
        job:   флаг отложенной отправки этой группы.
        Группа откатывается только целиком и только когда её никто
        не трогает — иначе правка одного ползунка сбрасывает соседний."""
        if job is not None:
            return
        vars_ = [v for v, _ in pairs]
        if not self.group_quiet(vars_, hold):
            return
        for v, val in pairs:
            if val is None:
                continue
            if int(val) != int(round(v.get())):
                self.sync_var(v, int(val))

    def sync_var(self, var, value):
        """Подтянуть ползунок И число рядом с ним из прошивки."""
        try:
            var._busy = True          # не даём command уйти в schedule_pid
            var.set(value)
            sv = getattr(var, '_sv', None)
            if sv is not None:
                sv.set(str(int(round(value))))
        except Exception:
            pass
        finally:
            try:
                var._busy = False
            except Exception:
                pass

    def schedule_rv(self):
        if self._rv_job:
            self.root.after_cancel(self._rv_job)
        self._rv_job = self.root.after(120, self.send_rpm_virt)

    def use_virt(self):
        """Переключить задатчик оборотов на виртуальный (с ПК).
        Якорь сбрасываем: он возьмётся из первого же кадра телеметрии,
        и отсчёт «резистор тронули» пойдёт от текущего положения ручки,
        а не от той, что была при прошлом уходе на ПК."""
        self.sp_src = 'virt'
        self._pot_anchor = None
        self.send_rpm_virt()

    def use_pot(self):
        """Переключить задатчик оборотов на реальный потенциометр (RP).
        Отменяем отложенную отправку RV — иначе виртуальный ползунок
        перекроет команду через 120 мс и вернёт прошивку в виртуальный режим."""
        if self._rv_job:
            self.root.after_cancel(self._rv_job)
            self._rv_job = None
        self._pot_anchor = None     # положение резистора на момент ухода на ПК
        self.sp_src = 'pot'
        self.send_cmd('RP')

    # ================= Интерфейс =================
    def start_ui(self):
        # --- Сверху: панель инструментов (две строки, чтобы всегда влезало) ---
        toolbar = tk.Frame(self.root, bg='#1a202c')
        toolbar.pack(side=tk.TOP, fill=tk.X)

        row1 = tk.Frame(toolbar, bg='#1a202c'); row1.pack(fill=tk.X)
        row2 = tk.Frame(toolbar, bg='#1a202c'); row2.pack(fill=tk.X)

        tk.Label(row1, text='Двиг.:', bg='#1a202c', fg='#eab308',

        self.btn_hb.pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=2)
        self.btn_rs = tk.Button(row1, text='СБРОС', command=self.do_reset,
                                width=6, bg='#ef4444', fg='white')
        self.btn_rs.pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=4)

        # Индикатор двигателя: красный при вращении, синий в покое.
        self.lbl_motor = tk.Label(row1, text=' ДВИГ ВЫКЛ ', width=12,
                                  font=('Arial', 11, 'bold'),
                                  bg='#1f2937', fg='#60a5fa',
                                  relief='solid', bd=2)
        self.lbl_motor.pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=8)

        # --- Задатчик оборотов: резистор или задание с ПК ---
        tk.Label(row1, text='Обороты:', bg='#1a202c', fg='#eab308',
                 font=('Arial', 10)).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=(10, 4))

                                  width=6, bg='#4b5563', fg='white')
        self.btn_virt.pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=2)

        tk.Label(row1, text='Защита:', bg='#1a202c', fg='#eab308',
                 font=('Arial', 10)).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=4)
        self.btn_p0 = tk.Button(row1, text='0', command=lambda: self.send_cmd('0'),
                                width=3, bg='#4b5563', fg='white')
        self.btn_p0.pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=1)
        self.btn_p1 = tk.Button(row1, text='1', command=lambda: self.send_cmd('1'),
                                width=3, bg='#4b5563', fg='white')
        self.btn_p1.pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=1)
        self.btn_p2 = tk.Button(row1, text='2', command=lambda: self.send_cmd('2'),
                                width=3, bg='#4b5563', fg='white')
        self.btn_p2.pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=1)
        self.btn_p3 = tk.Button(row1, text='3', command=lambda: self.send_cmd('3'),
                                width=3, bg='#4b5563', fg='white')
        self.btn_p3.pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=1)

        tk.Label(row2, text='ПИД:', bg='#1a202c', fg='#eab308',
                 font=('Arial', 10)).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=4)
        self.btn_pa = tk.Button(row2, text='Авто', command=lambda: self.send_cmd('a'),
                                width=5, bg='#4b5563', fg='white')
        self.btn_pa.pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=2)

        nb.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)
